- **261106.3** (2026-11-06) — Regional net position: exports shown negative, imports positive (and the 1 h change to match); short region names where the panel is narrow.
- **261107.1** (2026-11-07) — `#embed=freqhist`: the frequency history overlay alone, compact bar, no title / note / close; on `#embed=freq` the ⤢ history button is shown and asks the wall to open its history panel.
- **261107.2** (2026-11-07) — `AXB()` (8/3 px in any `#embed=` view) / `fqAx()`: wall frames' axis labels larger (frequency trace and histogram, history plot, at-home rain / temperature, pressure), gutters widened; trace frame edges unlabelled and history labels every 0.2 Hz when crowded; histogram x step at least 48 px.
- **261107.3** (2026-11-07) — `radar_alert` with the approach-episode keys (spoken once, by the RAIN voice when it is on).
- **261107.4** (2026-11-07) — At-home plots: "Precipitation at home" title; its scale to 64 mm/h (64 grid line), stretched to the next doubling when a bar is higher; temperature plot gets the "measured" / "forecast" labels above its top edge, as the precipitation plot.
- **261107.5** (2026-11-07) — Gas price chart: `gasSuspect()` (SMP buy < SAP / SMP sell > SAP) values as hollow dots, out of the axis range and the line, edge label when off the scale, legend note, "?" in the figures; `gasPubTxt()` publish time in the heading ("latest" when over 2 h old); first time label clear of the start labels.
- **261107.6** (2026-11-07) — Frequency histogram: linear time scale (was log), grid lines at a round step (1, 2, 5, 10, 15, 30 min, 1, 2, 3, 6, 12 h), any non-empty bin at least 2 px.
- **261108.1** (2026-11-08) — Grid balance bar `#gbBal` at the right of a new `.hdr-row2` (with `#hdrSummaries`): `gbBal` block (same as the wall's) — `feed()` from the fast frequency poll, `grid()` from the snapshot (`frequency`, `warnings`, `price`), `calc()`, `mount()`; canvas bar with zones, trail and needle; click scrolls to the frequency card; not in `#embed=` views.
- **261108.2** (2026-11-08) — `wxRainDraw()`: linear rain-rate scale (was log 0.1 .. 64), top the next doubling above the highest bar (2 .. 512 mm/h), grid lines at quarters, bars at least 1.5 px (`yBar`).
- **261108.3** (2026-11-08) — `wxRainDraw()`: scale starts at 0 .. 32 mm/h (was the next doubling from 2).
- **261109.1** (2026-11-09) — `tipAt(tip,e,dx,dy)`: hover cards placed beside the pointer, flipped above / left at the window edges; used by the generators treemap (`#genTip`) and the Octopus charts (`#cgTip`: bar, carpet and calendar tips). Frequency-history tip (`#fhTip`) kept inside its plot at the left / right ends.
- **261109.2** (2026-11-09) — Capacity margin card: radial gauge (`drawGauge`, `#marginGauge`) replaced by the margin ladder (`renderMarginLadder()`, `.mg-*`, `mgNotices()`, `mgGw()`; reliable margin from `d.reliable_margin`, declared headroom from `d.margin.current_mw`, hatched-gap tooltip via `balShowTip`). `drawMini24(series, now, notices)`: reliable margin −24 h to the end of the forecast, zones at 1 / 3 GW (were 2 / 4 GW lines), notice windows shaded, forecast low labelled; title "Reliable margin · last 24 h and NESO forecast".
- **261111.1** (2026-11-11) — Gas page: third view `hist` on `.gas-view-tabs` (`#gshView`, `gshFetch()` `/api/gas-daily` every 5 min while open, `gshRender()` table, `gshDraw()` lines with the gap shaded; `#gasview=hist`); flow diagram view box cropped by `GF_TRIM_T` 36 / `GF_TRIM_B` 62 (`gfCrop`, not when expanded).
- **261111.2** (2026-11-11) — Gas tiles: `.gk-y` bottom line filled by `gshTiles()` (yesterday's figure + `gshSpark()` 7-day sparkline: supplied, demand, difference as bars, closing linepack `lp_close`); `/api/gas-daily` now fetched whenever the gas page is drawn (5 min).
- **261111.3** (2026-11-11) — Gas tiles order: supply, demand, NTS linepack, supply − demand (linepack and the difference swapped).
- **261111.4** (2026-11-11) — Supply − demand tile: sub-line "linepack packing" / "linepack drawing down" (the bracketed "(demand > supply)" removed, so it stays on one line).
- **261111.5** (2026-11-11) — Gas tiles: sparklines smaller (44–76 px wide, 20 px high) and beside the text from about 1000 px page width (they drop below only when under 44 px is left, as on a phone); linepack tile reads "yesterday 359.7 mcm" like the others (its closing level; the tooltip says so); an estimated figure drops "mcm" to make room for its tag.
- **261111.6** (2026-11-11) — Supply history: `GSH_SPANS` 7 / 14 / 31 switch (`gshSpan`, `gbGshSpan`, `gshSetSpan()`; `gshDays` sliced from the month the server sends, so a shorter span redraws without a fetch); chart labels every `lblEvery` days with the month shown at each change, dot radius and the per-day difference labels set by the spacing; `.gsh .tb` scrolls under a sticky heading; `gshTiles()` always uses the last 7 days.
- **261112.1** (2026-11-12) — `wxT850At()` (the home forecast's hourly 850 hPa temperature nearest a time) and `wxPrecipShare(tw, t850)` (snow 1 / sleet 0.5 / rain 0; wet-bulb alone when there is no 850 hPa value); 850 hPa row under *Feels like* with its word (snow / sleet / rain / snow aloft / ice risk) and tooltip; used by `wxRainDraw()` for the measured and radar-track slots and by `wxTempDraw()`'s shading.
- **261112.2** (2026-11-12) — Local weather block: the word before the figure on the 850hPa and Apparent rows (was after it); label "850hPa".
- **261112.3** (2026-11-12) — Wind panel: the gusts line amber (`#f5b942`), matching the gust tick on the dial.
- **261112.4** (2026-11-12) — 850hPa row: the word made conditional ("rain if any", "snow if any", "sleet if any"), so a dry night no longer reads as a report of rain.
- **261112.5** (2026-11-12) — 850hPa row: `wxWetNear()` — the word is shown only when there is precipitation about (the engine's area state, the radar track here, anything measured at home in the last half hour, or a forecast within two hours); dry, the row is the temperature alone.
- **261112.6** (2026-11-12) — `gasDrawFlowEased()` (`GF_EASE_MS` 1500, `GF_EASE_FPS` 15, `GF_PREV`, `gfSnap()` / `gfKeys()` / `gfLerp()`): a poll eases every flow, total, linepack and imbalance figure from what is on screen to the new payload, drawing the first frame at the old values; structure changes, reduced motion, `gasNodeToggle()` and view switches draw in one step. `gbSlideHeight(el, apply)`: a block whose height changes is slid between the two heights (used by `gasLadderRender()` so a wrapping badge row no longer jumps the page); `.gas-ladder .gl b` in tabular numerals.
- **261112.7** (2026-11-12) — Flow waves: phases accumulated (`GF_PH_CONN` / `GF_PH_SPINE` / `GF_PH_TS`, elapsed ÷ speed, capped at 200 ms a frame) instead of timestamp ÷ speed, so the spine no longer jumps cycles each time its speed changes during an eased update.
- **261112.8** (2026-11-12) — Flow waves: `paint()` split out of the animation frame and called as the drawing is built, so freshly created segments and spine slices carry their opacity at once instead of drawing at full strength until the next frame (every redraw flashed).
- **261112.9** (2026-11-12) — Alarm badges made independent of the flow drawing: `gasProbeLast` caches the probe payload and `gasRender()` redraws the badges from it at once (they were filled only by a later fetch, so any re-render blanked them until the next poll); the flow draw is wrapped in try/catch and logs, and the probe fetch is started before the render so a drawing error can no longer skip it; `paint()` tolerates a wave without segments; `gbSlideHeight()` finishes a slide already running before measuring.
- **261112.10** (2026-11-12) — LNG column: CARGO ARRIVALS strip (31 gas days, a lozenge per unloading episode with a pip per day, days since on the right). "Last cargo" names the terminal and uses its own tank gain, on the flow diagram and the gas storage view.
- **261112.14** (2026-11-12) — Tanks box grows to fill the column down to the lanes, with `gsDrawTankDetail()`: send-out now and its share of supply, the window's total into the tanks against the window before, and a tank-stock sparkline over the same 31 gas days with its range and net change.
- **261112.13** (2026-11-12) — Cargo lanes moved down level with the LNG nodes and hanging below the first, so the bands into the nodes no longer cross the other sources' labels; one text row per terminal (the name carries the send-out, a terminal's connection points move to its tooltip) because the nodes are about 35 units apart; the stack no longer overruns the bottom of the drawing. A **CARGO ARRIVALS** header sits above the first lane.
- **261112.12** (2026-11-12) — LNG column rebuilt: a block per terminal (name, days unloading with a trend arrow on the window before, send-out, and a 31-day lane with day ticks) whose lane runs on as a tapered band into the terminal's node at the main ribbons' width scale; the diagram's own LNG labels are dropped while the column is open and the column is drawn under the diagram so the bands pass behind the other labels; `gsDrawCargoStrip` replaced by `gsDrawLngFlow`.
- **261112.11** (2026-11-12) — Cargo strip: the lozenge is centred on the middle of the days it covers rather than drawn from the first day's left edge, so the day pips sit symmetrically within it; a one-day cargo, whose lozenge is widened to the dot's minimum, was the worst case.
- **261114.7** (2026-11-14) — Gas panel's view switch relabelled and reordered: **GB System flow | GB Supply history | Gas storage**, so the two GB views sit together and the scope of each is on the button.
- **261114.6** (2026-11-14) — `forecast minimum` column right-justified, like the rest of the table's figures.
- **261114.5** (2026-11-14) — Fix: the Europe aggregate and the GB estimate are not GIE country series and are never forecast, so they show a dash with the reason on hover instead of sitting on "standby · updating" for ever.
- **261114.4** (2026-11-14) — Forecast plot calls out going under the alert level: the band below 5% is shaded, the stretch of the projection at or under it is drawn in the alert colour, and the day it crosses and the day it empties are marked with a dated rule. The minimum's own label turns red and bold and lifts clear of the 5% line when it is below it.
- **261114.3** (2026-11-14) — A country with no forecast yet reads **standby · updating** rather than a dash, so a store still backfilling its history looks like what it is; the plot sub-line drops its note about the band.
- **261114.2** (2026-11-14) — Forecast plot sub-line says what the band is (the spread of the analogue winters, padded to the range that held nine in ten past outcomes) rather than that it widens with distance, which the plot already shows.
- **261114.1** (2026-11-14) — By-country table: `forecast minimum` column (bars narrowed from 46% to 30% to fit) with the earliest date, the band and the analogue years on hover; clicking a country name opens `gsvFcPlot()` below the table — median, uncertainty band, 5% line and the minimum marked — and the choice survives a refresh.
- **261114.9** (2026-11-14) — `~` on an extrapolated forecast, with the reason, the record's range and the scaled reading in the tooltip; the plot's sub-line says so too, and the column note limits the quoted backtest accuracy to in-range countries.
- **261115.1** (2026-11-15) — `gfAllLayout()`: the LNG section puts its first node 400 below the section top (was 205) and spaces the terminals 76 apart (was 60), so in the wall's expanded view `gsDrawLngColumn()` grows the tanks box and `gsDrawLngFlow()` hangs the cargo lanes, instead of falling back to text-only rows.
- **261115.2** (2026-11-15) — Expanded wall view: `gfAllText()` draws the columns' text under 20 units `GF_ALL_TXT` (1.25) larger; `GF_CWW` 820 -> 900; storage rows' bar / % / detail at +180 / +385 / +400 (were +150 / +345 / +360), detail 12; LNG section first node 250 below the section top, terminals 60 apart (261115.1's 400 / 76 shrank the drawing ~15%); cargo date axis 26 below the last lane in one-line rows (was 15).
- **261115.3** (2026-11-15) — Frequency histogram: `fhgFade(col, age)` colours each bar by the age of its newest reading (`last[]`): mixed `FHG_GLOW` (0.45) × f towards white, alpha f, f = 1 − age / period.
- **261115.4** (2026-11-15) — Frequency histogram redrawn every 1 s while the page is visible (readings still merged once a minute and on each new burst), so the age fade is smooth.
- **261115.5** (2026-11-15) — Fade redraw every 15 s (the reading interval) instead of 1 s.
- **261118.1** (2026-11-18) — Storage forecast wording: the note under the plot cut from one 190-word run to three short paragraphs (what the forecast is, how accurate it is and what ~ means, what the colours mean), with `.gsv .note-s p` spacing; the plot's extrapolation sub-line now reads “lower now than any past winter on this date (…) — band widened to suit”. The ~ tooltip is unchanged.
- **261118.2** (2026-11-18) — `WSPD_U` with `wFromMph` / `wFromKmh` / `wSpdMph` / `wSpdKmh`, set by the radar frame's pill and pushed back to it on load; the wind gauge draws its ticks at round km/h values mapped onto the unchanged mph curve, so the needle and the gust and steering ticks never move. `say()` rewrites “N miles per hour” for km/h.
- **261118.3** (2026-11-18) — The speed gauge did not follow the unit switch when it was clicked on the standalone `/forecast` page, which is a separate document and cannot message this one: `storage` events now carry the change between tabs as well, `eaRenderWindDials` re-reads the stored unit as it draws, and a failed re-render is logged rather than swallowed.
- **261118.4** (2026-11-18) — `gasAlertsHtml` / `gasAlertPaint` fill a `#gasAlerts` slot at the top of the gas page with the `domain=="gas"` alerts, in the flood warnings' markup and styling (`ea-flood-list` / `ea-flood-item`); `renderAlertbar` repaints it so the two stay in step.
- **261118.5** (2026-11-18) — The derived margin signal becomes `gasMarginAlert`, merged into `gasAlertsHtml`'s list and stable-sorted by level with the server's; the `gas-margin-banner` markup and CSS are removed.
- **261118.6** (2026-11-18) — The same white cap above 55 dBZ on the Precipitation-at-home bars, replacing the rain/sleet/snow word in the hover with “possible hail”.
- **261119.1** (2026-11-19) — `html.embed, html.embed body{overflow:hidden}`: the generic single-panel embeds lock the document, leaving only the overlay's own scroller.
- **261122.6** (2026-11-22) — `glLad()` works out where a reading sits on its own ladder and how far it is from the next threshold; `chip()` draws that as a `gl-bar` with a tick per level and a `gl-sub` line. A downward ladder's scale is inverted so a fuller bar always means worse. `GAS_LEVELS_CALIBRATING` / `GAS_LEVELS_WINTER` / `gasLevelsNote()` carry the calibration caveat in one place, used by the strip's heading and the alarm settings' description.
- **261122.7** (2026-11-22) — New embed `#embed=eustore`: the Europe · gas storage card alone (`html.embedeu`), with the UK card, the card's own title, the ⚙ GIE key button in the head and the sources footnote hidden. `#gsView` is top-anchored with its height left to the content, never `inset:0`, so the card measures itself and not the frame; a 1-second poll posts `{gbEuH}` to the parent on change (polled, because `#gsView` does not exist until the gas panel has been built), and a 30-minute `gsFetch(true)` keeps a frame that nobody clicks up to date.
- **261122.8** (2026-11-22) — Every type size on the Rivers & floods page is 1 pt (1.33px) larger: the page's own rules take `calc(<size> + var(--eaf,0px))` and `#eaOverlay` sets `--eaf`. The variable is undefined elsewhere, so the `.ea-*` bars and bodies shared with the other overlays keep their sizes, and it is zeroed under `html.embedwx` so the wall's weather panel is unchanged.
- **261123.1** (2026-11-23) — The flood list's badges are bold, and the **% of normal** badge is white on its red rather than near-black. Found while doing it: `.fi-local` (“near you”) sat before `.fi-tag` in the stylesheet and so lost to it — it had been drawing as a plain tag ever since `.fi-tag` was added. Moved after.
- **261123.2** (2026-11-23) — `speakSummary()` rebuilt: rain is spoken only when `situational.here` or an approach is live, with the direction, ETA and distance, then the rivers with the nearest one's distance, then “No rain heading your way” where there is something else to report; silent when there is nothing. `sumDist()` speaks distances in the page's own units, and `wSpeakSpd()` now converts plain distances as well as speeds. The summary timer belongs to `arm()` / `disarm()` rather than to `startCritRepeat()`, where it only ran while a critical latch was live.
- **261123.3** (2026-11-23) — The alarm trigger line: `trigSet()` / `trigFromAlarm()` / `trigInfeed()` put one short line in whichever bar is in front (header and every overlay bar), coloured and border-flashed by tier, cleared after five minutes. Louder replaces; equally loud becomes “Multiple alarms” naming each; quieter is ignored. Categories with a header badge of their own (`TRIG_SKIP`: power cuts, gas, floods, rain) stay out of it. `riskPip()` — the tone-with-no-speech case — names the infeed loss and its MW, grouping several within five minutes into a count and a total. Shared with the other windows through `gbAlarmTrig` + the storage event; clicking opens the page the alarm came from and clears it everywhere.
- **261123.4** (2026-11-23) — `rampFetch()` / `rampCheck()`: `/api/ramp` polled every minute — whether or not the alarms are armed, since the server logs on the call and the baseline needs the readings — and two minutes before a boundary whose scheduled change is above its minute's ninth decile (or 1,000 MW while there is no baseline yet) one silent notice goes in the trigger line. Once per boundary; no tone.
- **261123.5** (2026-11-23) — The gas page's four KPI cards go from four rows to three: `.gas-kpi` becomes a two-column grid with the sparkline (`.gk-sp`, its own box now) beside the figure and a `.gk-foot` row carrying the caption and the “yesterday” line across both columns — in the sparkline's column that long line set the column width and squeezed the figure to an ellipsis. The separator and its padding go with the row. Figure and sparkline take container-query sizes so a narrow card shrinks them rather than clipping the unit, and the imbalance percentage drops below 240px.
- **261123.6** (2026-11-23) — The NESO notice badge is a two-line box in the capacity margin heading's top right: `.mg-h2` makes the heading a flex row with the title in `.mg-h2t`, and `.mg-note` is a bordered block — what the notice is and how short on the first line, the window on the second. On the narrow right-hand column the single line overflowed, so the window wrapped out of the border and sat under the title. Several notices in force stack inside the one box, divided by a rule.
- **261122.5** (2026-11-22) — A storage site's status line is measured at the size it ends up, not the size it is drawn: expanded on the wall, `gfAllText` enlarges every label by `GF_ALL_TXT` afterwards, so a quarter of the width was unaccounted for and a withdrawing site's line ran into the column's end dots. Trailing detail is now dropped until it fits — “~N d at max” first, since the bar and percentage beside it already carry the stock — and the pie is scaled to the finished text and drawn only when both fit. Wide, the line shares the dot's row and keeps real clearance; narrow, it sits below the dot and keeps its full text and its pie.
- **261122.4** (2026-11-22) — `spate` / `spateFor()` on the flood list: rivers over `riverPct` 100% are collected from `eaData.stations` and matched to a warning's `river_or_sea` by normalised name; a local match adds the `.spate` class (red border, red inset edge, red tint, a “N% of normal” tag) and sorts first, ahead of plain local warnings.
- **261122.3** (2026-11-22) — `dmGauge` serves both kinds of right-hand gauge: a capacity pie keeps the sources' green-over-red, while demand against a severe winter keeps yellow-over-amber. Exports carry `cap` from the server (Moffat 39.5, BBL 22, IUK 61) and an idle route's circle is dimmed.
- **261122.2** (2026-11-22) — A pie per withdrawing storage site (flow against its published deliverability) leading each status line in the storage column, from a `capm` map built beside `live`; `dmGauge` draws the LDZ row's pie from `demand_ref.ldz_today` / `ldz_peak`, outside the value and clear of the ribbons, with `rLabW` widened to suit; the header caption becomes “severe-winter demand” and its tooltip separates the daily cold-year figure from the Winter Outlook's 1-in-20 peak day.
- **261122.1** (2026-11-22) — `html.ovl-lock` locks the document while any `*-overlay` carries the `open` class, so the dashboard's sub-pages no longer leave a second, useless scrollbar down the window. A `MutationObserver` per overlay element drives it; the scroll position is saved and restored around the lock.
- **261121.3** (2026-11-21) — Forecast horizons on the storage chart: `GSV_FC_LINE_D` 90 bounds the central line, its minimum marker and the level-crossing dots (the band is still drawn to the full horizon); `GSV_FC_FLAG_D` 62 bounds the “empty” / “under 5%” flags and the column’s empty badge; `gsvFcIn` / `gsvFcAhead` do the test and `gsvFcP` floors the central figure at 0. The column is blank past the line horizon and its heading flips to “forecast maximum” once most countries are filling.
- **261121.2** (2026-11-21) — The over-100% tooltip no longer claims a store's deliverability falls as it empties. Checked over 11–21 Nov, the published figures barely move; they are a maximum for the whole gas day, which a site can surge above for a few hours.
- **261121.1** (2026-11-21) — The demand pie's numerator becomes `demand_ref.today` — National Gas's hourly-revised forecast of the whole gas day — instead of the live instantaneous total, so both sides of the ratio are gas-day volumes; the caption gains a leading “today” and the tooltip names the forecast and says the figure beside the pie is a different quantity.
- **261120.7** (2026-11-20) — The demand wedge is banded on the printed percentage rather than fixed yellow: `gpdCol` gives green below 50%, yellow through the middle and red from 95%, and the caption under the figure takes the same colour from 50% up. With red now in use as a fill, the over-100% rim moves from red to amber — the one colour that still reads against a full red circle.
- **261120.6** (2026-11-20) — A source with no published capacity now shows nothing at all instead of a faint dash, which read as a control. The gauge column's width is still reserved for every source, so the pies stay aligned and the side columns' lines keep clearing them.
- **261120.5** (2026-11-20) — Room for the gauges: a source's value row drops from 12 to `GF_VAL_DY` 16 below its name, since the pie beside the figure is taller than the figure and was crowding the name above; the gap between a pie and its value widens 5 -> 6.5.
- **261120.4** (2026-11-20) — The demand gauge gets its own colours, `GPD_*`, so it is not read as the same measure as the supply pies: yellow for demand so far over amber for the headroom left, and a red rim past 100%.
- **261120.3** (2026-11-20) — A pie gauge beside the demand total, demand over the server's `demand_ref.cold` (1-in-20 for this date), with a "N% of a 1-in-20 cold day" caption under the figure and the cold / seasonal-normal / warm set on hover; amber rim and caption past 100%.
- **261120.2** (2026-11-20) — Capacity pie gauges on the flow diagram's supply sources. `gasFlowGroup` sums each group's `cap`, de-duplicated by the server's key (Grain's two berths share one deliverability) and withheld when a member with real flow has none; `gfPieWedge` / `gauge()` draw a green flow wedge from 12 o'clock over a red remainder, full with an amber rim past 100%, or a faint dash where nothing is published. `gfLab.gw` carries the gauge column into `gfLabW`, so the side columns' lines keep ending clear of the label block.
- **261120.1** (2026-11-20) — The risk panel's infeed-loss flag carries the verdict: “checking frequency…” in amber while `system_risk.loss_event` is pending, then **FALSE ALERT** in blue for 15 minutes with the time, MW, source and reason (largest 15 s fall on hover), or “✓ confirmed” with the fall. A false verdict does not pip.
- **261124.1** (2026-11-24) — `mgEmn()` reads an Electricity Margin Notice's own figures out of its text (shortfall, contingency requirement, MW excluded by constraints, the Maximum Generation line), each independently so the other notice kinds degrade to nothing. `mgEmnBlock()` renders them under the plot with the derated-vs-operational explanation; `drawMini24()` draws the excluded MW as a hatched band off the margin line within the notice window, the remainder as a dashed line, and the contingency requirement as a dotted floor. The shortfall is not plotted — it is not measured against this axis.
- **261124.2** (2026-11-24) — Same wording fix as `grid_wall.html` 261124.6: short of the margin held **above** demand, not “short of demand”.

### `powercuts.py`

Header history began at 260916.1; earlier builds are in the dated entries above.

- **260916.1** (2026-09-16) — Added NIE Networks (Northern Ireland) and ESB Networks PowerCheck (Republic of Ireland) feeds; national totals and history now span both.
- **260916.2** (2026-09-16) — Footnote attribution rebranded from "GB" to "UK & Ireland".
- **260917.1** (2026-09-17) — ESB list fetch: longer timeout (25s) + one short-backoff retry on transient errors (timeout/429/5xx) before the feed is flagged stale.
- **260917.2** (2026-09-17) — A feed's last good reading (incl. an empty 'no outages' one) is now served as 'stale' for up to STALE_GRACE (2h) of silence before it becomes 'down'. Fixes ESB dropping straight to 'down' when it failed after a legitimately empty reading.
- **260917.3** (2026-09-17) — History log now records national un_new (new UNPLANNED incidents/sample) and un_off (unplanned customers off) so the dashboard's storm/power-cuts alarm can track non-planned cuts and restorations. Older rows lack them; readers tolerate.
- **260926.1** (2026-09-26) — Build history moved from the file header to CHANGELOG.md.
- **260929.1** (2026-09-29) — Decompress gzip responses sent without being requested (ESB list endpoint).
- **261006.1** (2026-10-06) — Frozen-feed detection: unchanged incident set for 3 h of daytime → `stale` with reason, `frozen_since`; history rows flag `frozen`.
- **261014.1** (2026-10-14) — Monitored postcode areas: `powercut_watch.json` store, postcodes.io validation and place names, `watch_matches()`; NPg incidents keep the postcodes of all collapsed rows.
- **261014.2** (2026-10-14) — UKPN: restored and `MULT-` group rows dropped; unconfirmed counts null with `count_pending`; planned customer figure; full postcodes kept for monitored-area matching.
- **261014.3** (2026-10-14) — NPg: finished incidents dropped, planned flag from `natureofoutage`, pending counts. ESB: finished planned outages dropped, times to ISO, `end_unknown`. NGED: planned works window.
- **261014.4** (2026-10-14) — SPEN: planned works past their end time dropped, planned start date used. NIE: times converted to ISO local.
- **261014.5** (2026-10-14) — Incidents > 7 days past their ETR moved to `overdue` (uncounted; ids kept in new/cleared diffing).
- **261015.1** (2026-10-15) — Monitored areas: optional `alias` per area (stored, cleaned, passed in matches).
- **261024.1** (2026-10-24) — Monitored areas: optional `repeat` per area (stored, passed in matches).
- **261024.2** (2026-10-24) — "Republic of Ireland" renamed "Ireland" in source texts and comments.
- **261025.1** (2026-10-25) — SPEN locations checked against their postcode districts; moved to the district group's centre when over 35 km from all of them (`pos_note`, `pos_src`, `pos_published`).
- **261025.2** (2026-10-25) — Postcode-district lookups per poll raised from 40 to 250, so a fresh cache fills on the first poll.
- **261025.3** (2026-10-25) — OpenDataSoft feeds read through the export endpoint in one call (gzip), NPg filtered to live rows on the server; paging only as a fallback.
- **261025.4** (2026-10-25) — Incidents inside another operator's licence area and over 5 km outside their own flagged `outside_area` with a `pos_note`.
- **261025.5** (2026-10-25) — A stored key is used for open feeds too (NPg, UKPN).

### `rain_probe.py`

Header carried only the current build; builds before 260923.1 are in the dated entries above.

- **260923.1** (2026-09-23) — Approach episode: one approach voice, reading-based timing, episode and shadow-threat logs.
- **260923.2** (2026-09-23) — Steering wind, speed gate, hand-over tracks, gauge flags. Never deployed (file lost); rebuilt as 260926.1.
- **260926.1** (2026-09-26) — Stage 2 rebuilt: steering flow used throughout, speed gate, hand-over tracks from wet-now gauges, stale tracks, stationary/suspect gauge flags, shadow-log steering fields; sea-net OC4 fallback reads upwind-first with rotation, keeps last readings with ages, labels the real source. Unused local removed from `select()`. Header tidied to the current build + CHANGELOG pointer. Gauges keyed by EA reference and named by place (else grid reference); points carry id/ref/grid/place.
- **260927.1** (2026-09-27) — `OPEN_METEO_MODELS` sent as `models=` on the rain-net and steering requests.
- **260927.2** (2026-09-27) — Local-first Open-Meteo with automatic public fallback (`_om_request`), 5-min local cooldown and retry, all-null detection, `local_down`/`local_up` logging; public-only backoff; `OM-local` metering; `om_get_json()` and `om_status()` for the server.
- **260927.3** (2026-09-27) — Land-probe limits relaxed while self-hosted Open-Meteo is serving: no episode cap, 15-min heartbeat/clear-check, all points per heartbeat, third range at 25 km, quiet-hours stretch ignored.
- **260929.1** (2026-09-29) — Radar-sampled sea net / home ring / land probes (RainViewer) with Open-Meteo fallback; late EA gauges (>45 min) excluded from the situation field, all-late treated as EA unavailable.
- **260929.2** (2026-09-29) — Late-gauge threshold 75 min with 60 min all-late hysteresis; home ring keeps its cache, samples immediately on the free sampler, labels by real source.
- **260929.3** (2026-09-29) — Open-Meteo current amounts converted to mm/h using the response interval.
- **260929.4** (2026-09-29) — Virtual net/ring points keep `modelled: true`; new `measured` flag for radar/OpenWeather readings.
- **260930.1** (2026-09-30) — Radar net ignores the quiet-hours multiplier; mobiles keep source and radar frame time (confirmed for RV or OC4); approach ETA corrected for the lead reading's age (`data_age_s`).
- **261014.1** (2026-10-14) — Rainfall prediction fine tuning: radar at home counts as arrival; "fizzled out" only when radar shows the upwind sector clear (`fizzle_held` otherwise).
- **261022.1** (2026-10-22) — "At your location" intensity measured at home (radar box at home / nearest gauge < 5 km, two-reading smoothing) instead of the 40 km 90-min peak; at-home heartbeats 20/60 min; re-entry hold and upgrade window 30 min; hourly heavier-rain-nearby note.
- **261022.2** (2026-10-22) — Alert audit fixes: rain at home also from radar (two frames); area words from current measured readings, "Continuous rain across the area", quiet after rain at home, 20-min re-announce floor; nowcast lines from measured home reading, gauge confirm within 5 km, visibility-only fog line, no wind/persistence claims; all-clear only when the area is clear.
- **261024.1** (2026-10-24) — Forward nowcast "heavier" message: time from the minute series (`intensify_eta_min`), "soon" when unknown, "Rain is continuing at your location…" after 30 min of measured rain at home (`home_wet_since`); edge trigger keyed on a stable identity so the countdown doesn't re-speak.
- **261030.1** (2026-10-30) — `radar_grid()`: the newest RainViewer frame as 0.5 km rain-rate cells around home, with a motion vector from the frame before (`_rg_motion()`, correlation) or the steering flow; in `situational["radar_grid"]`.
- **261104.1** (2026-11-04) — Max-rate history: `maxh_radar()` (each new frame's `_rg_max()` within `RG_KM`; one-off background back-fill of past frames via `_rg_grid(tile_fn=…)`), `maxh_gauges()` (physical gauges within the plot, 5-minute slots, 15-minute readings), `maxh_public()` → `situational.max_hist`; `rain_max_hist.json`.
- **261104.2** (2026-11-04) — `maxh_home()`: radar box at home and nearest gauge within `SIT_HERE_KM` into the max-rate store (`home_radar` / `home_gauge` in `maxh_public()`).
- **261104.3** (2026-11-04) — `_rv_newest_frame()`: frame list every `RV_MAPS_DUE_S` once a frame is due, else `RV_MAPS_TTL_S`; records publication / first seen (`_rv['pub']`, on the grid as `pub` / `seen`); `radar_view_grid()` with the engine's last flow.
- **261104.4** (2026-11-04) — `om_get_json(..., public_only=True)` / `_om_request(public_only)`: public host only, for variables the local instance doesn't sync.
- **261105.1** (2026-11-05) — `_rg_motion_multi()` (`RG_PAIRS` 3, `RG_AGREE_KMH` 8, `RG_AGREE_FRAC` 0.35): motion `src` radar / uncertain / steering; `_rg_motion_grid()` per-frame 1 km grids (`_rg['mgrids']`); `_rg_fetch_tile()`; `steer` on the grid.
- **261105.2** (2026-11-05) — `radar_cell_motion()` / `_rg_patch_motion()` (`RG_CELL_BOX_KM` 12), `radar_area_motion()`; `_track_motion()` radar first, verified by `_gauge_motion()` agreement or `_motion_hits()` (`TRACK_HIT_KM` 6, `TRACK_HIT_MIN_MOVE` 4, 45 min); approaching by path miss ≤ `SIT_APPR_MISS_KM` 8; tracks carry `move_src`, `verified_by`; `sit_probe_hist`, mobile `hist`.
- **261105.3** (2026-11-05) — `approach_episode.threat` adds `eta_lo`, `eta_hi`, `measured`, `snow`, `too_far`.
- **261105.4** (2026-11-05) — `_eta_say()`: spoken arrival (about N min within 30 min; clock time when measured; minutes on the steering wind); `eta_say` on the threat, used by the alert and confirm phrases.
- **261105.5** (2026-11-05) — `RG_HAIL_DBZ` 55 / `RG_HAIL_MMH`: hail cells as byte 255, grid `hail` summary.
- **261105.6** (2026-11-05) — `radar_hail_cells()`; threat `hail` (cone, ≤ `APPR_MAX_KM`); `sit.hail_here` (≤ `HAIL_HERE_KM` 3); "possibly with hail" in the alert, confirm and at-home phrases.
- **261106.1** (2026-11-06) — Radar track at home: `_rg_home_track()` per new frame when the area motion is measured (`RT_*`: 5-min slots to frame + 2.5 h, disc radius 1 km + 3.25 km/h of lead, ≤ 160 km upwind, mean and wet share), `radar_home_track()` → `situational.home_track`.
- **261107.1** (2026-11-07) — `radar_grid()`: one byte per cell is now the whole dBZ (0 = dry, 10–65; `enc: "dbz"`), was mm/h × 10 capped at 25.4 with hail as 255.
- **261107.2** (2026-11-07) — Radar watch: `radar_watch()` (`RW_*`), `_rw_describe()`; track slots carry the p75 of their wet cells; `ProbeState.rw_*`; an approach alert while a radar episode is live is dropped (logged `appr_alert_dropped`); `situational.radar_watch`; episode log events `radar_alert`, `radar_end`.
- **261112.1** (2026-11-12) — `temperature_850hPa` added to the hourly steering read (`fetch_om_steering` returns `t850`); `_SFC` (wet-bulb from `run_probe`'s new `temp_c` / `dew_c`, 850 hPa temp, 3 h staleness) and `precip_type()` -> (type, hedge, ice), the model's own snow flag winning where it exists; `_rw_describe(slots, kind, hedge)` with snow and sleet wording and a "Possible …" hedge; `radar_watch()` speaks the type and `ICE_CLAUSE`; `_appr_alert_phrase()` adds `ICE_CLAUSE` to a rain alert when the surface is freezing; `ptype` / `hedged` / `ice` in the `radar_alert` episode log.
- **261115.1** (2026-11-15) — `radar_frames(lat, lon)`: every past RainViewer frame around home as the radar view's grids (whole dBZ, as `radar_grid`), oldest first; each frame's grid built once (`_ra`, per home; the live grid reused when it is the same frame) on its own lock `_ra_lock`; tiles metered `radar_tile_anim`.
- **261115.2** (2026-11-15) — `radar_frames(lat, lon, hours)`: frames from the rain recorder's saved tiles first (`RA_REC_DIR`, `_ra_recorded()`), RainViewer only for a frame it lacks and still lists, else left out (`_RaMissing`); 1-24 h (`RA_MAX_H`); grids cached by frame time per home (up to `RA_KEEP` 160); each frame carries `rec`.
- **261115.3** (2026-11-15) — `_ra_motion()`: each frame's drift to the next (`mv`: vx, vy, src pair / near), measured by `_rg_motion` on 1 km grids (`_ra_coarse`, from the dBZ bytes) for pairs up to 20 min apart, else the median of measured pairs within two steps; cached per pair (`_ra["mot"]`).
- **261116.1** (2026-11-16) — Approach alert radar gate (`radar_sector_mm`, `APPR_GATE_RADAR_*`, `sector_mm` on `run_approach_episode`); steering ETA window 0.45–0.85× (`_steer_eta`, `STEER_ETA_*`); `_eta_age_corrected` wording; radar watch quiet after radar rain at home (`RW_AFTER_WET_S`, `ProbeState.rw_home_wet_ts`) and "weakening" phrase with time.
- **261118.1** (2026-11-18) — `RG_WIDE_KM` / `RG_WIDE_CELL` (100 km at 2 km): `radar_grid` returns a `wide` block beside the fine grid and `radar_frames` a `b64w` per frame with `wide_km` / `wide_cell_km` / `wide_n`. Both cuts come from the same tiles; a frame whose recorded tiles don't reach that far simply goes without, and never fails for it.
- **261118.2** (2026-11-18) — `_wide_watch()` scans the coarse surround upwind beyond `RG_KM` (rings of 5 km, ±60°, `WW_MIN_KM2` with `WW_NEXT_FRAC` behind it) and returns distance, bearing, band, area, closing speed and ETA; carried as `radar_grid().wide.watch` and `situational.wide_watch`, with a `wide_watch` template that speaks only when the nearer engines are silent.
- **261119.1** (2026-11-19) — `approach_threat` projects every cell along the steering flow before judging it: `along` and `miss_km` per cell, cells past or missing by more than `APPR_MISS_KM + APPR_MISS_FRAC × range` dropped before the peak, width and counts are taken, the lead chosen by `along` rather than range, and every ETA computed from `along`. `along_km` and `miss_km` ride along in the threat for diagnosis.
- **261119.2** (2026-11-19) — `approach_episode` publishes `qualifies_ok` beside the existing `qualifies` reason, and the threat it publishes now carries `along_km` and `miss_km`.
- **261120.1** (2026-11-20) — The `wide_watch` spoken line carries its distance in **miles**, rounded to the nearest 5 (floor 5), matching the miles-per-hour the other templates already speak; the Forecast view rewrites it to kilometres when km/h is the chosen unit.

- **261105.4** (2026-11-05) — Portrait panel: `cy=min(H/2,R+70)`; key scale `bw` 22–36 to fit, units label on its own line when narrow.
- **261105.5** (2026-11-05) — Light / dark theme block, `#themeBtn` in the titlebar (top-level only); `rgBuild` uses the mapped key colours.
- **261105.6** (2026-11-05) — Theme lightness map darker for text and lines.
- **261105.7** (2026-11-05) — Theme: panels ~15 % darker, tones desaturated; radar circle filled darker grey in light.
- **261105.8** (2026-11-05) — Portrait margin `MG` 32 (landscape 70).
- **261105.9** (2026-11-05) — `mvSrc()`: cell movement source / confirmation in the plot tag, hover and tracked-cells list.
- **261105.10** (2026-11-05) — `homeNow()` / `drawHomeNow()`; portrait circle 58 px lower.

- **261105.13** (2026-11-05) — Hail cells white (unthemed), key "hail?" swatch, "· hail nearby"; `cd()` countdown; 15 s redraw.

- **261105.15** (2026-11-05) — Tall layout only when height ≥ 1.3 × width (`PT`).
- **261107.1** (2026-11-07) — `AXW`: on the wall (top window /wall), the max-rate plot's axis labels 2 pt larger and the box a little bigger.
- **261107.2** (2026-11-07) — Rain-rate scale switch on the key (`RG_SCALE` 8 / 19 / smooth, `fvRainScale`; `colAt()`, per-byte colour table in `rgBuild()`); reads the grid's `enc: "dbz"` (older mm/h × 10 grids still draw).
- **261107.3** (2026-11-07) — Max-rate plot: scale to 64 mm/h with a 64 grid line, stretched to the next doubling (up to 512) when a bar is higher; bars coloured on the key's chosen scale (`colAt`).
- **261108.1** (2026-11-08) — Max-rate plot: linear scale (was log), top the next doubling above the highest bar (2 .. 512 mm/h), grid lines at quarters, bars at least 1.5 px.
- **261108.2** (2026-11-08) — Max-rate plot: scale starts at 0 .. 32 mm/h (was the next doubling from 2).
- **261104.2** (2026-11-04) — `placeTip()` keeps hover text inside the window; max-rate plot draggable by its title row (`fvMhPos.<shape>`, double-click resets) and hideable (`fvMhHidden`).
- **261104.1** (2026-11-04) — `drawMaxHist()`: max rain rate bar plot, bottom right (1h / 6h / 24h, radar / gauges / both, `fvMhSpan` / `fvMhSrc`); bar hover; redrawn each minute.
- **261031.1** (2026-10-31) — Radar overlay palette: rain green to red, sleet pinks, snow blues (Andy); opacity 45–75 %.
- **261030.2** (2026-10-30) — Radar overlay: `radar_grid` drawn under the plot at up to 50 % (75 % from 261031.1), coloured by rain rate (`RR_STEPS`, snow / sleet palettes by wet-bulb at home), moved to now by its motion; key with click toggle (`fvRadarProj`).
- **261030.1** (2026-10-30) — Radar-only mode draws at the frame's shape (`fitEmbed()`: 760 units high, width to the frame's proportions), so the plot stays round.
- **261112.1** (2026-11-12) — `t850Now()`; `precipKind()` takes the 850 hPa temperature with the wet-bulb (same rule as the dashboard's `wxPrecipShare`), so the radar's snow / sleet colours and the nowcast noun follow both; the key's unit line names both figures.
- **261115.1** (2026-11-15) — ▶ animate radar (top right): `ANIM`, `animLoad()` (`/api/radar-frames`), `animState()` / `animDraw()` (frames slid along the motion and cross-faded additively on `anLay`; projection AN_PROJ × 10 min along the motion when usable, amber dashed ring), `animTick()` (~30 fps, holds on the newest frame and at the end), `drawAnimCtl()` (play / pause, speed, ■ live, scrub timeline); the key's time line follows the animation; a new frame while animating fetches the frames again.
- **261115.2** (2026-11-15) — Animation span button 2 / 6 / 12 / 24 h (`AN_H`, `fvAnimSpan`), passed as `hours`; speeds 1, 2, 4, 8, ½×; long loops: hourly timeline ticks, the start day on the timeline, ages in hours.
- **261115.3** (2026-11-15) — `animTick()`: no hold on the newest real frame; the end-of-loop hold only after a projection.
- **261115.4** (2026-11-15) — `animState()`: each step slides along its own frames' drift (`frames[k].mv`) instead of the live motion; the key's animation line gives the drift then (~ when borrowed).
- **261115.5** (2026-11-15) — `anMv()`: projection motion falls back to the median of the last three measured pair drifts (`src: "recent"`) when the live motion is not usable.
- **261115.6** (2026-11-15) — `anMv()` back to the live motion only (261115.5's recent-drift fallback removed), with `anWhyNoProj()` shown in the panel; span buttons 2h / 6h / 12h / 24h replace the cycling one, wrapping to a second row on narrow panels.
- **261116.1** (2026-11-16) — Radar animation holds 5 s on the final frame (`AN_END_HOLD`), with or without a projection.
- **261118.1** (2026-11-18) — `mph | km/h` pill under the nowcast box (`drawSpdSwitch`, `U_BTNS`), `SPD_U` shared with the dashboard by localStorage and postMessage; every speed on the plot, in the HUD and in the would-speak lines goes through `spd()` / `spdSpeak()`. Seconds countdown to a firm arrival inside ten minutes, with a 1 s redraw gated on `CD_FAST`.
- **261118.2** (2026-11-18) — Follows a unit change made in another tab (`storage` event), not only one messaged in by the page it is embedded in.
- **261118.3** (2026-11-18) — Draws the coarse surround under the fine grid in both paths, clipped by `rgOutside()` to the circle minus the fine grid's footprint (even-odd) so the translucent fine cells keep their own colour; the animation composes each frame's two layers on a scratch canvas first, so the cross-fade's `lighter` pass doesn't add one to the other.
- **261118.4** (2026-11-18) — `drawWideWatch()` puts an amber chevron and distance/ETA on the rim at the heads-up's bearing with the detail on hover, and `wwTxt()` adds a `heads-up (100 km)` row to the situation table.
- **261118.5** (2026-11-18) — `HAIL_MMH` (55 dBZ through Marshall-Palmer): the max-rate bars are overdrawn white above it, and the hover reads “possible hail”.
- **261118.6** (2026-11-18) — Every font drawn on the radar canvas is 1 pt larger — ring and cell labels, the nowcast box, the key, the animation panel and the rate plot. The rate plot's box grows with them (minimum width 270 → 300, height 182 → 186) so its chip row still fits across a narrow embedded panel.
- **261118.7** (2026-11-18) — Nothing on the plot runs off the canvas now that the type is a point larger. Cell labels go through `labCentred()`, which slides a label in just far enough to stay whole and steps its size down only when it could not fit at all; a cell's motion source is dropped from the plot label (it stays on hover) where the full one would take more than 60% of the width. In the key block the units line wraps like the others — `wrap()` now falls back to filling by words when a part has no · to break on — and the colour swatches give up width before the scale switch does, so the switch always has its place.
- **261118.8** (2026-11-18) — `BG_MAPS` / `drawBgMap()` / `drawBgSwitch()`: browser-fetched raster tiles under the radar, zoom from the plot's metres-per-pixel, tiles placed by their Mercator corners, per-layer opacity, `BG_TILES` holding at most 400 images for the session, and the credit appended to the key block's wrapped lines.
- **261119.1** (2026-11-19) — Cell labels avoid each other, not just the canvas edge: `labCentred()` keeps a box per label drawn and tries, in order, the full wording where it belongs, the short one there, the short one a line away, then the full one a line away. Two cells close together therefore drop their motion source (still on hover) before either label moves off its cell, and a cell on its own keeps the full wording — the box is 14 px tall so a cell's own id, 15 px above its tag, is not counted as a clash.
- **261119.2** (2026-11-19) — The heads-up chevron keeps off the plot whenever the nowcast line already has something nearer — rain at home, or an arrival window — which is the rule its spoken line already followed. A band an hour out read as a contradiction beside “very heavy rain in 9 min”. It still shows against a dry or model-only headline, and the situation table carries it either way.
- **261119.3** (2026-11-19) — `labCentred()` takes an array of lines and plates each one (`LAB_PLATE`, `LAB_LH`), with `labPlate()` for the few labels drawn outside it; cell tags built as [motion, source]; `drawCardinals()` after the cells, muting any that clash with a registered label box; spokes extended by `CARD_OUT` with the outside stub at a stronger alpha.
- **261119.4** (2026-11-19) — The plates sat off their text: they assumed a middle baseline while the cell labels inherited `textBaseline='top'` from the rate plot. `labCentred()` now sets its own alignment and baseline instead of taking whatever was last used, and `labPlate()` reads the caller's to place the rect where the text will actually land.
- **261119.5** (2026-11-19) — Label plates get 2 px more above the text, so the margin matches the one below. Line spacing goes 16 → 20 px and the nudge steps with it, so stacked plates abut exactly instead of overlapping into a darker seam where two semi-transparent ones meet.
- **261119.6** (2026-11-19) — The tracked cells are hidden while the animation runs — circles, arrows, ids, tags and their hover targets. They describe where the rain is NOW, so over a frame from two hours ago, or over a projection of the next hour, they said something the picture underneath did not. They come back on ■ live.
- **261120.1** (2026-11-20) — Distances follow the speed switch: `dstU` / `dstN` / `dst` / `dstA` / `dstRings` / `dstTxt` / `dstSpeak` beside the `spd*` set. Rings 5/20/40 km or 3/12/25 mi (the outermost clamped to `KM`); the heads-up marker and its card, `fromHome`, `wwTxt`, the tracked-cell rows, the approach row's range and miss, the headline's edge distance, the hail distance, "moved to now", *nearest wet* and the *heads-up* row label all convert, and the quoted spoken lines are run through `dstSpeak` as well as `spdSpeak`.
- **261119.7** (2026-11-19) — `apprTxt()` adds an **approach** row to the situation table: whether the threat qualifies to speak, the gate that withheld it, and the threat's range, rate, class, miss distance and ETA.
- **261123.1** (2026-11-23) — `gbTrig` receiver: the alarm trigger line in the control bar. A single-purpose window, so clicking it only clears.

### `reservoirs.py`

New module at 261016.1.

- **261016.1** (2026-10-16) — Reservoir storage for the EA page. Sources: EA national monthly regional table (PDF, via optional `pypdf`), weekly drought summaries, area summaries, flood-monitoring reservoir gauges (sanity-checked) and the London Datastore. Drought status is replayed from the report text. Background build every 6 h (gauges hourly), with a cache and a daily gauge log.
- **261016.2** (2026-10-16) — South West Water reservoir-levels page (total and eight reservoirs with SWW's dates), logged by published date to `reservoir_company_log.jsonl` for history.
- **261016.3** (2026-10-16) — Water companies from Stream open data (ArcGIS feature services, read incrementally when a layer changes, with new layers found by catalogue search) plus company websites (Severn Trent weekly pages, South West Water, Northumbrian, Essex & Suffolk, United Utilities, Bristol Water), merged into `reservoir_store.json` with a daily backup. Per-region capacity-weighted totals; per-source status for monitoring; `series=` for one reservoir's full history.
- **261024.1** (2026-10-24) — "Republic of Ireland" renamed "Ireland" in the module notes.
- **261029.1** (2026-10-29) — South West Water website: reads "levels are at" as well as "level is at"; no reservoir rows is now reported as a failure.
- **261029.2** (2026-10-29) — South West Lakes Trust weekly table (reservoirs South West Water's page doesn't list); reservoirs silent 90+ days left out while the company has current ones.

- **261029.16** (2026-10-29) — `post_b6()` keeps the page's pinch-point alerts (`_pinch_store()`, validated, at most 30); `pinch_alerts()` returns them while under 5 minutes old.

- **261029.14** (2026-10-29) — ETYS ratings (`etys.py`) matched to the network's lines and sent with /api/wall-network (`ratings`, `ratings_info`); unnamed nodes' nearby names offered as second-choice names.
- **261029.13** (2026-10-29) — `_measured()`: NGED live GSP imports matched to nodes (aliases; old, all-zero and shared-operator nodes left out), `meas` in /api/wall; embedded units behind a measured GSP left out of the reconciliation; `_gsp_log()` / `_gsp_check()` score the model's unmeasured estimate against the measurements.

- **261029.11** (2026-10-29) — `_emb_now()`: NESO's embedded wind and solar estimate shared over the nodes by Embedded Capacity Register capacity (registered fraction only), `emb` in /api/wall; NESO embedded capacities read with the demand split.
- **261029.10** (2026-10-29) — Scotland's share of transmission demand fitted from NESO's Demand Data Update (half-hour x weekday/weekend + embedded wind and solar, refitted every 3 h) and sent as `split` in /api/wall; `post_b6()` keeps the page's modelled transfer per half-hour (`b6_log.json`) and checks it against NESO's metered Scottish transfer.
- **261029.9** (2026-10-29) — Gunfleet Sands 3: own landing at Waterglade, Clacton (GFS3 newsletter); a landing within 2 km of where another farm's line to the same node starts ends the line there ("to" kind "land").
- **261029.8** (2026-10-29) — Export cables mapped part way out from the connection (LINK_SEA_KM 15 km): line from the farm to the cable's sea end ("to"), one farm per cable; NODE_RENAME for Sizewell, Heysham, New Deer, Crystal Rig.
- **261029.7** (2026-10-29) — Offshore connection audit: OFFSHORE_UNITS (Hornsea 3 → Norwich Main, Walney 2 → Stanah, Walney 3, Burbo Bank 1 → Birkenhead); local-network landings ("near") for 12 small farms; nearest node never another wind farm's substation; sources noted per entry.
- **261029.6** (2026-10-29) — Offshore links follow a farm's mapped 132 kV export cable (OpenStreetMap or operator) to where it ends; Gwynt y Môr → Bodelwyddan.
- **261029.5** (2026-10-29) — `/api/wall-dnolines?kv=`; OpenStreetMap 132 kV lines an operator's map covers are left out of /api/wall-network; Northern Powergrid live readings by node in /api/wall (`local_live`).
- **261122.1** (2026-11-22) — `_constraint_day()`: the day's tightest half-hour and, per group, the window it is forecast to spend over its limit, from rows `_constraints()` already fetched and threw away. `constraints_fetch` records each attempt (time, ok, rows, last good, error class) and travels with it, so a wall panel with nothing to show can say whether that is the grid or the feed. Both ride in `boundaries.day`.
- **261123.1** (2026-11-23) — `_constraint_day()` keeps only TODAY's rows. The NESO file is day-ahead and the query asks for everything from today 00:00, so tomorrow's half-hours came back too: counted as one day they gave groups “75 hh” of a 48-half-hour day and drew two days on top of each other on the midnight-to-midnight bar.
- **261123.2** (2026-11-23) — `_next_boundary()`, a `bnd` horizon in `T`, and `_ramp_public()`: every unit's notified or instructed level at the next :00 / :30 less its level now, summed, with generation and interconnectors kept apart and the biggest few contributors named. `get_ramp()` serves it from the same cached payload the wall uses.
- **261123.3** (2026-11-23) — `_bnd_log()` / `bnd_log.json`: per half-hour, per constrained boundary, how many runs saw it, the highest percentage of its limit it reached, the most turned down behind it, and whether that limit was NESO's or ETYS's. 60 days, pruned like the other logs, written every 5 minutes. `post_b6()` takes either the old list of names or the new objects. Header build corrected — it still read 261030.4 after three changes.
- **261124.1** (2026-11-24) — `_constraint_day()` also returns `times` and `grid`: every group's forecast flow as a whole percentage of its limit for each of today's half-hours, for the panel's heat grid. Built from rows already fetched, today only (the file is day-ahead), capped at 999 — about 880 bytes.
- **261124.2** (2026-11-24) — `B6_LOG_DAYS` 4 → 30; `post_b6()` stores `[total, ac, dc]` when the page sends the split and a bare total otherwise; `_b6_check()` reads both shapes and adds the mean Western Link share of the modelled transfer.
- **261125.1** (2026-11-25) — `_constraints()` fetches from yesterday 00:00, and `_grid_for()` (factored out of `_constraint_day()`) builds a half-hour grid per day: `times`/`grid` for today as before, `times_prev`/`grid_prev`/`day_prev` for the completed day.
- **261029.4** (2026-10-29) — `/api/wall-local` (embedded generation per node from `dno_data.py`); offshore farms not in `OFFSHORE_LINKS` use their DNO register GSP; unit rows carry registered capacity (Elexon).
- **261029.3** (2026-10-29) — `OFFSHORE_LINKS`: offshore wind farms' connection points (TEC register, published project information, mapped export cables); `offlinks` in /api/wall-network and each link's output in /api/wall; the farms' units counted at their connection.

- **261112.1** (2026-11-12) — LNG panel: "last cargo" names the terminal and quotes its own tank gain rather than the UK-wide net.
- **261122.1** (2026-11-22) — The Gas alarm levels panel carries each measure's ladder, as the dashboard's strip does: `glad()` places the reading on its own scale, the row gains a `glb` bar with a tick per threshold and a `gls` line giving the levels and the distance to the next one, and a downward ladder's scale is inverted so a fuller bar always means worse. `GAS_LEVELS_CALIBRATING` / `GAS_LEVELS_WINTER` / `GAS_LEVELS_NOTE()` replace the fixed “derived · first-pass levels” caption; flip them with the pair in `grid_dashboard.html`.
- **261122.2** (2026-11-22) — “How gas demand is being met now” becomes an accordion: `GMET_OPEN` / `GMET_KEY` hold the open group by label, the group rows carry `data-gm` and a chevron and act as buttons, and only the open group's entry points are drawn.
- **261122.3** (2026-11-22) — The gas column becomes an accordion of its own: `gasSideRender()` keeps **Gas system now** open at the top and renders the rest as `.gsec` heading bars, one of which opens at a time into a `.gp.gopen` panel that takes the remaining height and scrolls inside itself (`gbWallGasOpen.<mode>.<side>`). The chip selectors are gone, the alarm rows are back to one line (`.gbadges` flex row: label, headroom, a 64px `.glb` bar, value, thresholds moved to the row's `title`), and `gmetbar()` is merged into `gmet()` so the split bar heads the table it describes.
- **261122.4** (2026-11-22) — Both accordions go multi-open: `c.open` is a list of panel keys and `GMET_OPEN` a Set of group labels, each persisted with `-` standing for “none open”, so a deliberate all-closed survives a reload while “nothing remembered” still opens the first panel / biggest group. `.gasside` scrolls, an open panel's own `h2` carries a `u.gx` chevron that collapses it, and `gsys` takes `flex:1 1 auto;min-height:150px` so it grows into spare height without being squeezed away.
- **261122.5** (2026-11-22) — Collapsed headings carry the panel's full title and a `GAS_SUM[k]()` summary line (`.gsl` / `.gsv`, with the level classes colouring it); `GAS_S`, the short-label map, is gone. The heading bar wraps, so a long title and a long summary stack rather than collide. The alarm summary counts only the measures at the worst level and always names an official notice in force, whatever its level.
- **261122.6** (2026-11-22) — Gas panels size to their content: `.gasside .gp` and `.gp.gopen` take `flex:0 0 auto` with no floor, `.gp.gopen .body` goes back to `overflow:visible`, the `gsys` grow rule and the `has-gmet` special cases are gone, and `.gasside .gp .gcv` gets a fixed 180px so a plot still has a height of its own. The column's own `overflow-y:auto` carries the overflow.
- **261122.7** (2026-11-22) — `gasSidesOn()` builds ONE gas column, `colL || colR` with the full `GAS_L.concat(GAS_R)` list; the both-sides branch that split the panels into `GAS_L` on the left and `GAS_R` on the right is gone.
- **261122.8** (2026-11-22) — `geu()` becomes the dashboard's own Europe card in an iframe (`#embed=eustore`), built once per open so a refresh does not reload it; `GEU_H` and a `message` listener size the panel from the height the frame reports.
- **261122.9** (2026-11-22) — `colStack(LAY.stack)` turns a column of panels into an accordion: the slots keep their own elements and renderers but leave the stage's absolute layout for a scrolling `.colstack`, `list[0]` never collapses, and a panel's own `h2` closes it (`gbWallColOpen.<mode>`, `-` for all collapsed). `P_.boundaries` is rewritten with `today` / `now` views over `boundaries.day` (`bndWinHtml`, `bndTightHtml`, `bndGapHtml`), `P_.outages` takes `cfg.only` to pin one view and `P_.regions` is the net-position half with the region diagram; `regAbove()`, `RG_EXP_K` and the ⤒ expand button are gone and `rgDiagH()` gives the diagram a height where the panel has none.
- **261122.10** (2026-11-22) — The region diagram is sized from its width (`rgDiagH()`, 0.8 × width, 420–820px) instead of a flat 480, and `.rgdw` takes that height outright rather than `flex:1`, which in a panel with no spare height resolved to nothing and cropped the diagram at the panel's edge. It is a map, not a chart: squeezing the height crowds the arrows between the region boxes, which is the part worth reading.
- **261122.11** (2026-11-22) — The 5120 layout's `outages` slot takes `only: "out"`. Listing both panels without pinning the pair left the second one on the remembered `gbWallOutView` setting, so a column that had been on *net position* showed it twice.
- **261122.12** (2026-11-22) — `COL_SUM[p]()` gives each collapsed panel in a `colStack` its headline item on the heading bar (`.gsv`, pushed to `REFRESH` so it follows the data): the boundary groups over a limit today or the tightest one, the biggest mover of the last 15 minutes, the biggest exporting region, and what is off with the largest single report.
- **261122.13** (2026-11-22) — `COL_SUM.regions()` takes the MAXIMUM of `R.net`, not the minimum: a positive value there is a region exporting, which `rgBars` prints with a minus and draws leftwards to match the interconnectors. Written from the panel rather than the data, the headline named the biggest importer and called it an export.
- **261122.14** (2026-11-22) — `COL2` holds the stacked column: `col2Set()` swaps it between the electricity accordion and the gas one, a `.colbar` above it carries the switch, and `gasSidesOn()` takes the column's box from `COL2` rather than measuring slots, which a stacked column no longer carries. `gasCol()` is factored out so both paths build the column the same way; `COL2_PEND` holds the regional panel's choice until the column exists, since the panels are built first.
- **261123.1** (2026-11-23) — `gbTrig` receiver: the alarm trigger line in the wall's header, in the gap before the clock, re-mounted when a layout change rebuilds the header. Clicking it switches the regional panel to the source the alarm came from.
- **261123.2** (2026-11-23) — `bndGW()`: the boundary summary lines read in GW like every other transfer figure on the wall. NESO publishes these in MW, so the peak had been printing “5469 / 1450 GW”.
- **261123.3** (2026-11-23) — The *today* view's header reads “tightest”, not “peak”, and carries the percentage: it is the worst flow-against-limit ratio of the day, and the biggest flow is usually a different group. Checked against NESO's own rows for the day — the figures were right, the word was not.
- **261123.4** (2026-11-23) — ETYS boundaries are drawn only while constrained and flash: `bndState().on` loses the 200 MW floor (any turn-down counts) and gains `hi`, which is that floor used as the colour instead — `BND_LO` amber under it, `BND_HI` pink over. The draw skips anything not in `BNDON`, so the faint dashed lines are gone from an unconstrained map; `pickBnd()` skips them too, since an undrawn line should not be hoverable. The `bnd` layer entry is removed from `LAYERS` and its `la("bnd")` gates with it.
- **261123.5** (2026-11-23) — Constrained boundaries: `BND_HI` pink → red, the tooltip drops the “over 200 MW, so pink” clause (true, but not label material), and the block pins `lineDashOffset = 0`. It had been inheriting the offset `drawDnoLines()` left behind, so the dashes travelled along the boundary and read as power flowing across it.
- **261123.6** (2026-11-23) — A constrained boundary carries its percentage of the limit in a boxed label near each end (`bndEnds()` walks 30 px in from the two ends in screen space, so the label sits on the map rather than out at sea; a boundary that closes on itself gets one), drawn after every line so the labels are never struck through. At **100 % or more** the line is twice as thick and the flash period halves (430 → 215 ms in the sine), so the ones actually over their limit stand out from the ones merely at 90 %.
- **261123.7** (2026-11-23) — The percentages are +2 pt and get out of each other's way: `drawBndPct()` runs at the very end of the frame, after the substation names, circuit values, site names, interconnector labels and the Scotland → England box have all claimed their space (`drawICLabels()` and `drawBorderLabels()` now record theirs), and each end tries five distances in along the line — 30, 58, 90, 126, 166 px — taking the first that is clear, or the least crowded if none is. Each placed label becomes an obstacle for the next. A boundary whose ends are both off the canvas, which a zoomed-in map can do, falls back to a sample of its visible stretch rather than going unlabelled.
- **261123.8** (2026-11-23) — `BND_HOT_NESO` / `BND_HOT_ETYS` (1.0 / 1.15) replace the flat `pct >= 1` for the doubled line: `bndState()` gains `neso` (the limit came from NESO's day-ahead feed) and `hot` (past that source's threshold). `bndReport()` sends `{n, p, s, d}` per constrained boundary to `/api/wall-b6` instead of a bare name, for the new `bnd_log.json`.
- **261124.1** (2026-11-24) — `trSubject()` / `trTabs()` / `TR_PICK`: the transfer panel takes the tightest constrained boundary, the border when there is none, or whatever the heading buttons or a map click chose; `trNote()` is the per-boundary note. `noteFold()` wraps a panel's long note in a remembered disclosure, and `slotReflow()` resizes the slot to what the panel needs and shifts the slots below it in the same column (skipped inside a `colstack`, which reflows by itself). The map's click handler checks `pickBnd()` before `pick()`. `trNote()` guards `N[u[1]]`, which `bndTip()` does not — an out-of-range unit node there would blank the tooltip.
- **261124.2** (2026-11-24) — `mgEmn()` reads a margin notice's own figures; the capacity margin header shows the shortfall and the MW constrained out, `mgEmnTip()` puts the derated-vs-operational explanation on hover, and `marginPlot()` draws the excluded MW as a hatched band off the margin line inside the notice window, the remainder dashed, and the contingency requirement as a dotted floor. The dashboard's block markup was tried here first and clipped: the slot is 200 px with 20 px of column to grow into.
- **261124.3** (2026-11-24) — The notice's explanation becomes a fold in the capacity margin panel, its button on the heading line (`noteFold()` takes a label; the delegated handler now accepts a button that sits outside its `.nfold`). `slotReflow()` loses the column-room cap and calls the new `stageGrow()`, which extends the stage past `LAY.h`, top-aligns it and adds `#stageSpacer` so the page can scroll to what was pushed down. Two measurement traps found and avoided: an auto-height pass under-reads a panel holding a plot sized at `height:100%`, and measure-and-correct against `scrollHeight` chases its own tail for the same reason (it ran to 1,382 px) — so the open block's own height is measured instead, and `.body.foldopen` stops the figures stretching into the new space.
- **261124.4** (2026-11-24) — Opening a fold no longer moves the wall: `fitStage()` keeps the stage's own vertical offset instead of top-aligning it when grown (that moved everything up 340 px on a 2560-wide window, which reads as the layout jumping rather than a panel opening), the spacer covers the offset as well as the height, and the opened block is scrolled into view. Checked at 2560×1400, 1920×1080 and 3840×1600: no movement, the block's top meets the plot's bottom exactly, fully visible without scrolling at all three.
- **261124.5** (2026-11-24) — The notice is a card floating over the wall (`#wpop`, `wpopShow()`/`wpopClose()`) instead of growing the panel, and the stage-growing machinery that came with 261124.3 is gone: `stageGrow()`, `#stageSpacer`, `html.grown` and the top-align in `fitStage()` all removed, and `slotReflow()` has its column-room cap back. Growing the wall past its own height made the page scroll as well as the wall, and two scrollbars on a wall display is worse than the problem it solved (Andy, 24 Nov). The card lives inside `#stage` so it scales with everything else, sits under its panel at the panel's width, lifts itself if that would run off the bottom, re-renders with the panel so its figures stay current, and closes on its ×, on Escape, on a click outside, or when the notice ends.
- **261124.6** (2026-11-24) — Wording: “short of the operating margin it wants to hold, not short of demand” → “short of the margin it wants to hold **above demand** — demand itself is covered”. Nobody is short of demand; the shortfall is generation, and what distinguishes it from a real supply problem is that it is generation wanted on top of demand rather than to meet it (Andy, 24 Nov).
- **261124.7** (2026-11-24) — The electricity|gas switch moves off its own bar and into the heading of whichever panel is at the top of column two: `COL2_BAR_H` and `.colbar` are gone, the `colstack` box starts at the column's own top again (74 px rather than 108 px on the 5120 layout, and 34 px taller), and `col2Switch()`/`col2Mount()` put the buttons back after every render — the gas accordion rewrites its own innerHTML, so a node carried around would be destroyed. The click is delegated for the same reason.
- **261124.8** (2026-11-24) — `TR_PICK = "all"` renders every constrained boundary on one list, ordered by `bndLat()` (a boundary's mean latitude, cached on it); `bndGridHtml()` / `BND_HEAT` draw the day's grid as the boundary panel's third view, dropping groups that never reach a limit and counting them in the key.
- **261124.9** (2026-11-24) — The `all` view repeats the single view's `kpi()` trio per boundary with per-boundary `tn()` keys, so each figure counts and tints on change like every other number on the wall; the bar rows it replaced did neither.
- **261124.10** (2026-11-24) — The single-boundary view's counting numbers get per-boundary token keys (`tr:bn:<name>` and so on). They shared one key, so switching boundary made each figure COUNT from the old boundary's value to the new one, and a glance mid-count showed a number belonging to neither — B4 reading “4.0 GW” while its own percentage said 91 % of 2.0 GW, which is how it was spotted. The percentage joins them as a counting figure rather than jumping.
- **261124.11** (2026-11-24) — The b6 report carries `ac` and `dc` beside the total, and `wlShare()` gains the note that the share splits the border flow rather than sizing it.
- **261124.12** (2026-11-24) — `trKpis()` builds one boundary's three figures for both the single and the `all` views: transfer and turn-down first, the limit last, and an ETYS capability shown as a plain uncoloured figure with its percentage in the caption. `bndState().hot` is now `neso && pct >= 1` and `BND_HOT_ETYS` is removed; `drawBndPct()` labels with the turn-down, appending the percentage only where the limit is NESO's.
- **261125.1** (2026-11-25) — `b6Err()` puts the scored mean absolute error on the border transfer's caption, and `trNote()` states that a NESO day-ahead limit is a forecast of the limit rather than the operative one.
- **261125.2** (2026-11-25) — The boundary panel's views become `prev` / `day` / `now`; `bndGridHtml()` takes a `pv` flag and draws either day (no “now” column on yesterday's, and its own note); the *today* windows view and its dead branch are gone. `BND_HEAT` is replaced by a ramp of strictly increasing luminance.
- **261125.3** (2026-11-25) — `BND_HEAT` becomes blue · dark red · light red · orange · yellow (Andy's sequence), five bands, luminance 46 → 60 → 105 → 156 → 214 so it still brightens all the way up. Over 250 % is no longer separated from 150 %. The key is prefixed “% of the group's limit”: the cell is Flow_MW ÷ Limit_MW, a ratio of two power figures, and nothing in the panel is energy.
- **261125.4** (2026-11-25) — `BND_HEAT` becomes a continuous ramp interpolated between `BND_STOPS` — blue, dark red, light red on the limit, orange, yellow, white at 500 % — instead of five flat bands: yesterday's grid goes from 5 distinct colours to 145. Luminance rises monotonically across 0–600 % (checked at every 10 %). The key samples the same function at 25, 75, 100, 160, 280 and 500+, so the swatches cannot drift from the cells.

### `osm_sites.py`

New module at 261029.1.

- **261029.1** (2026-10-29) — New module: UK Grid Atlas's OpenStreetMap generator sites and BM unit map, fetched weekly into `osm_sites.json`; OSM unit positions laid over Terravolt's (missing filled, >1 km corrected, >20 km only with matching fuel and name).
- **261029.2** (2026-10-29) — Generator-site list for `/api/wall-sites` (position, fuel, capacity, name, operator, start; biggest first).
- **261029.4** (2026-10-29) — Each generator site carries its DNO register entry (`conn`: operator, GSP, BSP, kV) in `/api/wall-sites`.
- **261029.3** (2026-10-29) — REPD: newest quarterly CSV found weekly via the gov.uk content API, operational and under-construction GB projects kept in `repd_sites.json`, OSGB36→WGS84; matched to OSM sites (fuel, size-scaled radius, names or capacity); REPD-only operational projects added to `/api/wall-sites`.

### `dno_data.py`

New module at 261029.1.

- **261029.1** (2026-10-29) — New module: the six DNOs' Embedded Capacity Registers (Opendatasoft with the power-cuts keys, CKAN without), weekly into `dno_ecr.json`; GSP → network node by name; per-node embedded generation; site → register entry matching.
- **261029.4** (2026-10-29) — NPg live 132 kV: newest reading's time asked for first, readings within 40 minutes of it exported with a 120 s limit (the 90-minute window from now found nothing); `newest` in its status.
- **261029.3** (2026-10-29) — NGED live GSP data (about 55 files, every 5 minutes, background, 150 s limit): `nged_gsp()`, `nged_gsp_status()`.
- **261029.2** (2026-10-29) — The operators' 132 / 66 / 33 kV line maps (Opendatasoft GeoJSON, SSEN shapefiles, any shapefile in `dno_lines/`), monthly into `dno_lines.json`, simplified; Northern Powergrid live 132 kV circuit readings (every 5 minutes).

### `etys.py`

- **261029.2** (2026-10-29) — ETYS study boundaries from NESO's shapefile (monthly, standard library, Web Mercator → lon/lat, simplified; closed loops in two halves), cached in `etys_boundaries.json`.
- **261029.1** (2026-10-29) — New: transmission circuit ratings from NESO's ETYS Appendix B (newest found on NESO's page, monthly check), read with the standard library, cached in `etys_ratings.json`; matched to network lines by name, nearby name, connections, tees and intermediate nodes; plausibility range per voltage.

### `build_network.py`

New script at 261023.1.

- **261023.1** (2026-10-23) — New script: builds grid_network.json from the gb-dispatch-model network and OpenStreetMap names.
- **261023.2** (2026-10-23) — Adds interconnector cable routes (`icroute`) from links.csv.
- **261023.3** (2026-10-23) — Adds 132 kV distribution lines outside Scotland (`dist`), display only.

### `start_wall.bat`

New launcher at 261023.1.

- **261023.1** (2026-10-23) — New launcher: opens the wall on two monitors as Chrome/Edge app windows.

### `gas_storage.py`

New module at 261028.1.

- **261028.1** (2026-10-28) — New module: National Gas per-site storage flows, stock, LNG tanks and deliverability; estimated per-site fill; GIE AGSI+ Europe; GIE key test and storage.
- **261028.2** (2026-10-28) — 12-month average NTS demand (PUBOBJ1030) for GB days at average use.
- **261028.3** (2026-10-28) — Europe trend rows carry each of the previous five years' same-day % full.
- **261112.1** (2026-11-12) — Per-terminal LNG tank inflow (PUBOBJ2403/2411/2412), fetched over 40 days and turned into unloading episodes over a 31-day window (`uk.lng.terminals`); `uk.lng.last_cargo` now carries the terminal and its own tank gain.
- **261112.4** (2026-11-12) — `uk.lng.stock_series` (tank stock over the strip's own window, oldest first, gas-day shifted) with `stock_from` / `stock_to`, and per-terminal `gwh` / `prev_gwh` totals for the two windows.
- **261112.3** (2026-11-12) — Episodes now join consecutive days over 20 GWh and are kept only if the episode totals over 100 GWh, so a thin unloading day stays with its own episode while tank accounting is thrown out; 71-day fetch for the trend on the previous window; `terminals[]` carries `days`, `prev_days` and, per episode, a `gross` estimate (net plus the terminal's send-out from the days either side) and a `multi` flag where that says more than one cargo.
- **261112.2** (2026-11-12) — Fix: the per-terminal LNG fetch sat behind the main refresh's "due" check, so after a restart it waited for the next publication slot (up to 16 hours) before the cargo strip had anything to draw. It now runs on its own 6-hourly clock ahead of that check and reports a change of its own, so a restart picks it up within a poll.
- **261114.1** (2026-11-14) — `GIE_COUNTRY_FROM` backfills each country's GIE history to 1 Oct 2020 in `GIE_COUNTRY_DAYS` chunks within `GIE_BACKFILL_S` per refresh, with the oldest date asked for held in `cty_from` so a country that published nothing in a window is not retried forever.
- **261114.3** (2026-11-14) — Fix: the country backfill sat behind the GIE publication-slot check, so on an existing store it would not start until the next slot — up to half a day with one winter of history, too few analogues, and therefore no forecast at all. It now runs in `_gie_backfill()` on its own clock ahead of that check, every `GIE_BACKFILL_EVERY_S`, and reports a change of its own. The forecast cache key carries the length of the series as well as the gas day, so a "no forecast" worked out before the backfill finished is not held until tomorrow.
- **261114.2** (2026-11-14) — `_forecast_country()`: six analogue winters replayed from today's level, withdrawals paced against this season, median and padded band; earliest day of a flat bottom, the whole flat window, peak when filling, exhaustion dates, and a watch/alarm state. Cached per country per gas day; carried on each country as `forecast`.
- **261114.4** (2026-11-14) — Each analogue winter is now also carried scaled (today's level × that winter's own proportion of its start) beside the shifted version; `out_of_range` when today's level is below every analogue start, with `range_lo` / `range_hi` and `alt_min_pct`, and the band widened to span both readings when it is.

### `gas_storage.html`

New page at 261028.1.

- **261028.1** (2026-10-28) — New page: gas storage pop-up (UK figures, embedded flow diagram, Europe Trend / By country, GIE key window).
- **261028.2** (2026-10-28) — GB days at average use (table and neighbour tile); days labels name their basis.
- Retired at 261028.2 (2026-10-28): the view moved into the dashboard's gas page; `/gas-storage` redirects there.
